- [anthropic/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/anthropic/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Fast mode's `speed` parameter has no Settings field — Fast mode is GA with no beta header on Opus 5.5 (2026-10-15) and ships that way on Fable 5.2 (2026-11-09), where `speed: "fast"` buys up to 2.5x output speed at double the token price. `params.update(self._settings.extra)` already carries it to the request, so it is reachable; it is only untyped. Opus 5 and Opus 4.8 remain a research preview needing the `fast-mode-2026-02-01` header, which the hard-coded `betas` list does not send, and fast mode on Opus 4.8 is removed on 2026-11-30. — *needs a call: whether a parameter only three models accept, and that `extra` already reaches, earns a typed field*
- [anthropic/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/anthropic/llm/2026-11-09.md) — A plain dict in `Settings.thinking` fails at request time with `'dict' object has no attribute 'model_dump'` (since 2026-08-25, 10 weeks) — `Settings.from_mapping` coerces thinking dicts, but the dataclass constructor stores them uncoerced and both request paths call `model_dump()` on them (`llm.py:517` and `llm.py:606`); still no `__post_init__` on 24761a4c6 — *needs a call: whether the coercion belongs in __post_init__ so every path accepts the dict form that from_mapping documents*
- [anthropic/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/anthropic/llm/2026-11-09.md) — `temperature`, `top_k` and `top_p` are rejected with a 400 on Claude Opus 4.7 and later models (since 2026-08-25, 10 weeks) — the service forwards them through `extra_body` rather than as typed parameters, so the SDK no longer objects but the API still does; a user who carries settings across a model switch gets a failed turn rather than a warning. API version `2026-10-21` widens this to every model, so the SDK pin item above decides it too — *needs a call: whether the service should warn or drop them on models that reject them, or leave it to the provider's error*
- [assemblyai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Settings carry fields AssemblyAI no longer documents (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — continuous_partials, formatted_finals and word_finalization_max_wait_time are absent from the streaming spec and ignored by the server, and continuous partials are documented as unconditional behavior, so the default-on field is a no-op; the spec's parameter list no longer carries `language_code` either (only `language_codes`), and the deprecated field still has no ".. deprecated::" marker — *needs a call: these are public Settings fields, so retiring them is a deprecation cycle rather than a docstring fix — confirm the team wants to start one*
- [assemblyai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/stt/2026-11-09.md) — LLM Gateway post-processing of finalized turns (llm_gateway) is not exposed (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — JSON-stringified chat-completions config on the connect URL, now also the documented route to real-time translation; results arrive as LLMGatewayResponse messages the service does not parse — *needs a call: running an LLM inside the STT service overlaps with the pipeline's own LLM stage — needs a product call before any plumbing*
- [assemblyai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Streaming webhooks (webhook_url, webhook_auth_header_name, webhook_auth_header_value) are not exposed — documented connect params that POST the session's finalized turns to an HTTP endpoint after termination; they appear on the webhooks page but not in the AsyncAPI spec's parameter list, and a voice pipeline already holds every turn it would deliver — *needs a call: webhook_auth_header_value would travel in the websocket URL's query string — confirm the team wants a secret-valued Settings field, and that post-session delivery is worth exposing at all*
- [assemblyai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Turn-level and word-level speaker_confidence from diarization is dropped before it reaches the frame — the spec's TurnPayload and Word both carry an experimental `speaker_confidence` (0.0–1.0) when speaker_labels is on; `TurnMessage`/`Word` do not declare it, so pydantic discards it and the TranscriptionFrame's `result` never shows it — *needs a call: AssemblyAI calls the score experimental and uncalibrated, so confirm whether to surface it before its shape settles*
- [assemblyai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/stt/2026-11-09.md) — previous_context_n_turns docstring states a server default AssemblyAI has changed — the Settings docstring says "the server default, which is 3"; the streaming spec now says "When unset, the server default applies (currently 5 turns)". Pipecat sends nothing when the field is unset, so only the documentation is wrong — *needs a call: AssemblyAI changes server-side defaults without notice — confirm whether docstrings should quote them at all rather than just say "the server default"*
- [assemblyai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/stt/2026-11-09.md) — The Inworld realtime example still names assemblyai/u3-rt-pro as its transcription model (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — examples/realtime/realtime-inworld-locally-driven-turns.py sets stt_model = "assemblyai/u3-rt-pro"; this is Inworld's namespaced model ID inside its realtime session config, not a value Pipecat sends to AssemblyAI — *needs a call: whether Inworld accepts an assemblyai/universal-3-6-pro model ID is an Inworld question — belongs to the inworld/realtime unit*
- [assemblyai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Error categories lose detail the server's error_type already gives — `_handle_error` maps every non-retryable 1008 to AUTHENTICATION, so `insufficient_funds` reads as a credential problem; non-retryable errors after Begin (`invalid_request`, `input_rate_exceeded`, `audio_backlog`) all become UNKNOWN even though error_type names them — *needs a call: QUOTA is not in _PERMANENT_CATEGORIES, so mapping insufficient_funds to it would make the service reconnect into a session that cannot succeed — decide whether the category or the permanence rule moves*
- [assemblyai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The default voice is an opaque UUID rather than AssemblyAI's documented slug — `_DEFAULT_VOICE_ID` is 49c443a4-8a1d-5f7e-b70b-98bdbd9823c3; a probe confirmed `aai-audrey` resolves to that same UUID, and AssemblyAI's voices page lists slugs only. The same literal is repeated in four examples and in the CLI registry config — *needs a call: a cosmetic swap that touches four examples, the CLI registry and the docs page — confirm the team wants the churn, and whether the default should instead be AssemblyAI's own English default (aai-kyla)*
- [assemblyai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — sample_rate is passed through unvalidated, so an unsupported pipeline rate kills the service at setup — the API accepts 8000, 16000, 22050, 24000, 44100 and 48000; a probe at 32000 came back `3006 invalid_parameter` before Begin, which `_handle_error` categorizes INVALID_REQUEST — a permanent category, so the service is marked unusable for the rest of the call — *needs a call: the server's message is clear, so the question is whether to fail earlier with a client-side check or to resample — needs a call on which*
- [assemblyai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/assemblyai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — No HTTP TTS class for AssemblyAI's /v1/speech routes — AssemblyAI also exposes POST /v1/speech, POST /v1/speech/events (SSE with word timings) and OpenAI-compatible routes; Pipecat ships only the WebSocket class, where several providers ship an Http variant too — *needs a call: a new service class, not a settings change — confirm there is demand given the WebSocket session already covers the real-time path*
- [asyncai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/asyncai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `speed_control` and `stability` are not reachable from Settings (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — Documented HTTP request fields (`speed_control` number 0.7–2.0, `stability` integer 0–100) that `AsyncAITTSSettings` adds nothing for — it is an empty subclass of `TTSSettings`, and neither class reads `Settings.extra` when building a request. Async's model page still restricts both to `async_flash_v1.0`, so on the default `async_flash_v1.5` they are accepted and ignored, and the websocket init message has no slot for them at all. — *needs a call: a stance on exposing settings that the default model accepts but does not honor, and that the websocket class cannot send*
- [asyncai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/asyncai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The language map offers ten codes the default model does not support (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `language_to_async_language` maps 16 Language values, but per Async's model page `async_flash_v1.5` covers 6 (en, es, fr, de, it, pt) and only `async_flash_v1.0` covers the other nine; `nl` appears in neither the model page nor the HTTP reference's 15-code language list. A call with an unsupported code on the default model still succeeds, so a user gets no signal that it was ignored. — *needs a call: whether to warn per model, document the split, or leave the map model-agnostic*
- [aws/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Newer Converse request fields are not reachable: `serviceTier`, `Tool.systemTool` (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `serviceTier.type` is one of priority/default/flex/reserved and AWS positions priority as the fastest tier, but neither the default model nor Claude Haiku 5.5 accepts it in the probe account, so there is nothing to verify a pass-through against; `Tool.systemTool` is how Nova 2's built-in web grounding and code interpreter are requested, and the adapter only emits `toolSpec` tools — *needs a call: whether to add fields the probe account cannot exercise, or leave both until a model that accepts them is in reach*
- [aws/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Docs and docstring describe the service as Nova and Claude only, though every Bedrock Converse model works (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — Bedrock's text catalogue is 94 model ids including `openai.gpt-6.1-terra`, `xai.grok-4.7`, `moonshotai.kimi-k3.1` and `zai.glm-5.3`; the class docstring and docs.pipecat.ai name only Amazon Nova and Anthropic Claude, and the docs page's example still pins `us.anthropic.claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929-v1:0`, which Bedrock marks LEGACY — *needs a call: whether to widen the wording, and whether the docs change belongs in this repo or in the docs repo*
- [aws/other](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/other/2026-11-09.md) — Nothing in the repo exercises an AgentCore invocation (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `tests/test_aws_credentials.py` constructs AWSAgentCoreProcessor to check that its client signs with renewed default-chain credentials, but no test or example drives an LLMContextFrame through process_frame, and the AgentCore samples live in pipecat-examples. Nothing here can be run against a deployed agent, which is why this unit has no live probe. — *needs a call: Whether an AgentCore example belongs in this repo, given it needs a deployed agent runtime to run.*
- [aws/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — aws_sdk_bedrock_runtime is pinned below 0.10 while 0.12.0 is current (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — The pin `>=0.6.0,<0.10` resolves to 0.9.0; the ceiling is deliberate, because 0.10.0 renames `Config` to `AsyncBedrockRuntimeConfig` and `BedrockRuntimeClient` to `AsyncBedrockRuntimeClient`, the names `create_client()` imports. PyPI's latest is still 0.12.0 (2026-10-08), so the gap did not widen this cycle. — *needs a call: whether the 0.10+/0.12 rename migration is worth doing now or the pin holds until the SDK reaches 1.0*
- [aws/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — The SYSTEM_SPEECH content role is missing from the Nova Sonic adapter's Role enum (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — Nova Sonic accepts SYSTEM_SPEECH as the `role` on a text contentStart to pick the transcription script for Hindi code-switching (Latin / Devanagari / mixed); the enum in `adapters/llm/services/aws_nova_sonic_adapter.py` has only SYSTEM, USER, ASSISTANT, TOOL — *needs a call: a stance on whether Hindi script selection is worth a user-facing knob, and what it would be called*
- [aws/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — `endpointing_sensitivity` is typed `str` rather than the documented HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW set (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — a typo reaches the API instead of failing at construction; AWS documents exactly three values — *needs a call: whether to narrow the type now or keep it open for values AWS may add*
- [aws/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/stt/2026-11-09.md) — `transcript-format` (spoken vs written rendering of numbers and dates) is unreachable (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — AWS documents `x-amzn-transcribe-transcript-format` (`spoken | written`, default written) on StartStreamTranscription, but the presigned-URL websocket endpoint appears not to implement it: the 2026-09-14 run found a signed URL carrying `transcript-format=bogus` connected and transcribed normally, while `partial-results-stability=bogus` on the same endpoint was rejected with "Member must satisfy enum value set: [high, low, medium]". A Settings field would be a silent no-op. — *needs a call: Whether AWS honours `transcript-format` over `/stream-transcription-websocket` at all — worth an AWS support question before spending the plumbing.*
- [aws/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The SynthesizeSpeech LanguageCode parameter is not exposed (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — bilingual voices pick their language from LanguageCode, and the 2026-10-21 generative additions Arjun and Kiara are bilingual (en-IN and hi-IN); the service only emits an SSML `<lang>` wrapper built from the `language` setting — *needs a call: whether `language` should also feed LanguageCode or a separate field is added — the two can disagree*
- [aws/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/tts/2026-11-09.md) — OutputFormat is hard-coded to pcm, which Polly caps at 16 kHz (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — generative voices are natively 24 kHz (the generative-voices page states 24 kHz as their default sample rate) and only expose that through mp3/ogg_vorbis/ogg_opus; pipelines running at 24 kHz get 16 kHz audio upsampled by the service. Polly also added mulaw/alaw output on 2026-03-25 — *needs a call: reaching 24 kHz means decoding mp3 or ogg in the service; needs a call on the dependency*
- [aws/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/aws/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Speech marks (word timestamps) are not requested (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — SpeechMarkTypes returns word/sentence/viseme marks, but only as a separate json-format request, so word timing costs a second billed synthesis — and the generative engine does not produce speech marks at all — *needs a call: whether doubling Polly requests is acceptable for word timestamps*
- [azure/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Azure-only chat-completions fields (data_sources, user_security_context) have no named Settings field (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `extra` reaches the request body verbatim (`params.update(self._settings.extra)` in base_llm), so On Your Data and the Defender end-user context are usable today; not probed, since data_sources needs a provisioned AI Search index. This is discoverability, not capability. — *needs a call: whether `extra` stays the documented answer for Azure-only request fields*
- [azure/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — Azure's realtime transcription and translation session types are not reachable (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — Azure exposes dedicated flows for `gpt-realtime-translate` (`/openai/v1/realtime/translations`) and for transcription (`session.type = "transcription"`, models `gpt-realtime-whisper`, `gpt-live-transcribe`). The service only speaks the voice-agent session. — *needs a call: a call on whether these belong in Pipecat as a realtime STT/translation service rather than in this LLM class*
- [azure/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — docs.pipecat.ai has no page for AzureRealtimeLLMService (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `/api-reference/server/services/llm/azure` documents only `AzureLLMService` (200), and `/api-reference/server/services/s2s/azure` still returns 404, so the deployment-name rule for `Settings.model` and the v1-endpoint requirement live only in the class docstring. — *needs a call: the docs site is a separate repo, so this needs an owner there rather than a change here*
- [azure/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/stt/2026-11-09.md) — The `azure` extra floor (`azure-cognitiveservices-speech>=1.47.0`) is behind the SDK features this class could use (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — the floor predates the 1.48.2 CRL fix Microsoft required for Linux/Android by 2026-07-01 and the 1.52 features above (streaming model selection, commit boundary); fresh resolves already get 1.53.0, so only pinned-old installs are exposed — *needs a call: whether provider-watch may move dependency floors, or whether that is a maintainer-owned change.*
- [azure/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Azure's `en-Latn-IN` real-time locale has no `Language` enum member — October 2026 added a real-time model that returns romanized Indic transcripts for English, Hindi, Bengali, Kannada, Marathi, Tamil and Telugu without language identification. `STTSettings.language` accepts a raw string, so `Settings(language="en-Latn-IN")` reaches `SpeechConfig` today; only the typed enum path is missing — *needs a call: `Language` is shared by every service, so adding a member is a cross-service call rather than an Azure one.*
- [azure/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Personal voice / instant voice cloning is not reachable (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — the cloned voice is addressed with `<mstts:ttsembedding speakerProfileId='...'>` wrapping the text (supported by every MAI model including the 2.1 family, explicitly unsupported by DragonHD and DragonHDOmni); `_construct_ssml` has no element for it — *needs a call: it is gated access (Limited Access Review) and adds a nesting level to the SSML builder, so it needs a decision on whether Pipecat carries a feature most users cannot enable.*
- [azure/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The text streaming input API is not used, so synthesis starts only once a sentence is aggregated (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `SpeechSynthesisRequestInputType.TextStream` on the websocket v2 endpoint accepts LLM tokens as they arrive; Microsoft's sample writes chunks into `request.input_stream` — *needs a call: the streaming input accepts no SSML — only voice name and output format — so every `Settings` field this class exposes would be dropped on that path; whether that is a second mode or a replacement is a product call.*
- [azure/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The sentence-boundary silence is hard-coded at 20 ms (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `_construct_ssml` always emits `<mstts:silence type='Sentenceboundary' value='20ms' />`; Azure also defines Leading/Tailing/Comma-exact and the `-exact` variants, none of which a user can select, and both HD families ignore the element entirely — *needs a call: the current value is a deliberate low-latency choice for agents, so exposing it means deciding what the default stays and which silence types are worth a field.*
- [azure/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/tts/2026-11-09.md) — An unlisted pipeline sample rate silently yields 24 kHz audio tagged with the pipeline's rate (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `sample_rate_to_output_format` falls back to `Raw24Khz16BitMonoPcm` for anything outside {8000, 16000, 22050, 24000, 44100, 48000}, while `TTSAudioRawFrame` is built with `self.sample_rate`, so the audio plays at the wrong speed instead of failing — *needs a call: whether an unsupported rate should raise, resample, or keep today's silent fallback.*
- [azure/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The HD expression adapter declares six Omni-only styles for plain DragonHD voices — `_voice_expression_adapter()` returns one `AzureHDExpressionAdapter` for both `:DragonHDLatestNeural` and `:DragonHDOmniLatestNeural`, and its `_STYLES` list includes `ecstatic`, `joking`, `proud`, `relieved`, `shocked` and `terrified`, which Azure's HD voices page documents only in the Omni style table. Probed on `en-US-Ava:DragonHDLatestNeural`: `[ecstatic]` is absorbed (2.80 s of audio, same as no marker, and no extra word boundary), so the marker is never spoken — the delivery is simply not performed. — *needs a call: splitting the adapter per HD family means a second capabilities list and a second class, so it needs a call on whether a silently-ignored style is worth that.*
- [azure/voice_live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/voice_live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — MCP and Foundry-agent tools can only be passed as raw dicts, and their server events are dropped — api-version 2026-07-15 documents MCPTool (server_label/server_url/allowed_tools/headers/authorization/require_approval) and FoundryAgentTool alongside function tools. `VoiceLiveTool` is `FunctionTool | dict`, so a user can declare either as a dict, but `parse_server_event` models none of `mcp_list_tools`, `mcp_call`, `mcp_approval_request` or `foundry_agent_call`, and there is no way to send `mcp_approval_response`. With `require_approval: always` (the default) the session stalls silently. — *needs a call: whether provider-hosted tools belong in a Pipecat realtime service at all, since the approval round-trip needs new client events rather than a settings field*
- [azure/voice_live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/voice_live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — `avatar`, `animation`, `output_audio_timestamp_types` and `metadata` session properties are unreachable (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — All four are documented session fields dropped by `SessionProperties`. `metadata` and `output_audio_timestamp_types` are pure pass-throughs; `avatar` (now including photo avatars driven by vasa-1/vasa-2, with a `scene` object) and `animation` additionally need their server events (`response.video.delta`, `session.avatar.*`, `response.animation_viseme.*`, `response.audio_timestamp.*`) modelled and pushed, which `parse_server_event` currently returns None for. — *needs a call: whether avatar video output belongs in this service or in a separate processor, before the plain pass-through fields are worth adding on their own*
- [azure/voice_live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/voice_live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — Image input (`input_image` content part) is not modelled (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — `ItemContent.type` is `Literal["input_text", "input_audio", "text", "audio"]`; RealtimeRequestImageContentPart adds `input_image` with `image_url` and `detail` for user messages. — *needs a call: whether a realtime service should accept vision frames at all, and which Voice Live models support them*
- [baseten/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/baseten/llm/2026-11-09.md) — `reasoning_content` from prior assistant turns is not sent back, which the always-on-reasoning models Baseten now serves ask for — Baseten's Beam-1.0 note says to preserve `reasoning_content` from prior assistant turns in multi-turn conversations; Beam-1.0 and meta-models/muse-spark-open cannot turn reasoning off. The shared OpenAI chat-completions path neither surfaces nor replays that field, so multi-turn quality on those models is whatever the provider does without it. `DeepSeekLLMAdapter` is the existing precedent for a provider that needs the field on assistant messages. — *needs a call: Whether a thin wrapper should carry an adapter for models that are not its default; the fix is adapter work in the shared OpenAI path, not a one-line setting.*
- [bland/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/bland/tts/2026-11-09.md) — A turn over 4,000 characters is rejected with `context_overflow` and nothing in the service splits or warns (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — `speak.text` is capped cumulatively per `context_id` (4,000 chars) and `/v2/tts` at 5,000 per request; a long-form turn ends mid-sentence with an ErrorFrame — *needs a call: a stance on whether a long turn should be split across context_ids or the cap just documented*
- [camb/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/camb/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Camb's `inference_options` (stability, temperature, inference_steps, speaker_similarity) is not reachable from Settings (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — MARS-8-family-only tuning knobs, documented as unsupported on the two 8.1 beta models; accepted by the SDK's `tts()` but still absent from the endpoint's published request schema, which shows them in prose and an example only. — *needs a call: a stance on per-model applicability — the fields silently do nothing on the beta models*
- [camb/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/camb/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `enhance_named_entities_pronunciation` is not exposed (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — Top-level boolean on the streaming TTS request, default false, now documented as unsupported on the two 8.1 beta models; a one-field pass-through. — *needs a call: a live probe with the field set, which the rejected credential blocks*
- [camb/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/camb/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The 8.1 betas' inline non-verbal tags have no Camb expression adapter — The streaming endpoint documents inline controls in `text` for `mars-8.1-flash-beta` and `mars-8.1-pro-beta`: six non-verbal tags (`[laughter]`, `[sigh]`, `[confirmation]`, `[question]`, `[surprise]`, `[dissatisfaction]`) plus bracketed CMU phoneme overrides. - [gladia/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/gladia/stt/2026-11-09.md) — `RealtimeProcessingConfig.words_accurate_timestamps` is no longer in Gladia's live schema (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — The field is absent from api.gladia.io/openapi.json and the live init reference, but the endpoint still accepts it (201 this run). `/v2/live` validates strictly, so a user setting it breaks hard the day Gladia drops it. — *needs a call: whether to deprecate the field now or wait until Gladia actually rejects it*
- [google/gemini_live-stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/gemini_live-stt/2026-11-09.md) — Request path uses google-genai's deprecated `language_hints` and `language_auto` instead of top-level `language_codes` (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — both fields still carry "will be removed in a future version" in google-genai 3.0.0 — the major release did not remove them, so the deadline moved out again. — *needs a call: `language_auto=False` (disable auto-detection with no hints) has no equivalent under `language_codes`, so the migration drops a documented Settings behavior*
- [google/image](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/image/2026-11-09.md) — Image controls beyond aspect ratio and size are not reachable from Settings (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `types.ImageConfig` also carries `person_generation`, `prominent_people`, `output_mime_type` and `output_compression_quality`; `thinking_level` is reachable through `GenerateContentConfig.thinking_config` and Search grounding through `tools`, and the service sends neither — *needs a call: which of these users actually ask for — the thinking levels differ per model (minimal/medium/high on 2.1, minimal/high on 3.1 Flash Image) and are billed, so exposing them needs a stance on validation*
- [google/image](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/image/2026-11-09.md) — Google documents image generation only on the Interactions API — every example on the image-generation page is now `client.interactions.create` / `/v1beta/interactions`; the migration guide says generateContent "remains fully supported" with no removal date, and our generate_content calls succeeded 6/6 today, but multi-turn editing via `previous_interaction_id`, video input as context and image_search grounding are documented only on that surface — *needs a call: whether to keep the service on generate_content or plan a move to interactions, which is a rewrite rather than a settings change*
- [google/interactions-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/interactions-llm/2026-11-09.md) — `service_tier` is not a Settings field — Priority inference is an Interactions-API-only tier aimed at second-level, non-sheddable responses - squarely a voice-agent concern - and lists gemini-3.6-flash, gemini-3.8-flash and gemini-4-argon among its models. It is in preview and costs 75-100% more than standard, with rate limits at 0.3x. It is reachable today without a code change: `extra={"service_tier": "priority"}` passes straight through as a top-level request key and the probe below answered with it set. — *needs a call: does a dedicated field earn its place while the tier is in preview and `extra` already reaches it?*
- [google/interactions-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/interactions-llm/2026-11-09.md) — Interactions function results accept image, document, audio and video blocks, which the service cannot send — Added 2026-11-05 - a function_result step takes document, audio and video alongside text and image, inline or by URI. Pipecat returns function-call results as text/JSON through the Gemini Interactions adapter, so a tool that produces media has no way to hand it back. The same gap is open on google/llm. — *needs a call: does multimodal tool output belong in the universal LLMContext function-result shape before any one provider exposes it?*
- [google/interactions-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/interactions-llm/2026-11-09.md) — A continued (long-decode) response logs a spurious incomplete warning and reports only its last hop's tokens — google-genai's automatic_continuation defaults to True, and with stream=True each intermediate hop ends with an interaction.completed event whose status is requires_continuation before the next hop's events arrive. `_handle_event` warns 'response incomplete' for any status outside completed/requires_action, and `usage = ... or usage` keeps the last hop's usage, which the SDK documents as per-hop rather than cumulative. Out of reach at the service's 4096-token max_tokens default; a user who raises max_tokens past the per-request decode limit meets it. — *needs a call: should interaction.completed with status requires_continuation be skipped and per-hop usage summed, or is long decode out of scope for a voice pipeline?*
- [google/interactions-vertex-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/interactions-vertex-llm/2026-11-09.md) — google-genai 3.0.0 is outside the `>=2.25.0,<3` pin — google-genai 3.0.0 shipped 2026-11-06; the installed 2.34.0 is the highest the pin allows. For this class 3.0.0 is mostly inert - Vertex clients keep defaulting to v1beta1, which is also where Vertex serves Interactions long decodes - and its Interactions changes are CodeExecutionResultStep.result becoming a union, Office/OpenDocument MIME types, and preservation of unknown fields. Moving the pin is a repo-wide google-extra decision, not a change this unit can make alone. — *needs a call: when does the google extra move to `<4`, given 3.0.0 also switches the default HTTP client to httpx2 and the Gemini API default version to v1?*
- [google/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Gemini function responses accept audio, video and Office/OpenDocument content and file URIs, which the service cannot send — Added 2026-11-05 (google-genai `FunctionResultSubContent`). Pipecat returns function-call results as text/JSON through the Gemini adapter, so a tool that produces an image or a file has no way to hand it back to the model. — *needs a call: does multimodal tool output belong in the universal LLMContext function-result shape before any one provider exposes it?*
- [google/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/llm/2026-11-09.md) — `media_resolution` is not a Settings field on GoogleLLMService (since 2026-08-25, 10 weeks) — Gemini 3 documents `media_resolution` for image/document/video input, and GeminiLiveLLMService already exposes it. It is reachable today through `extra` without a code change. — *needs a call: does a dedicated field earn its place when `extra` already passes it through, and should it be top-level only when Gemini 3 also accepts it per content part?*

- [google/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — `_DEFAULT_THINKING_LEVEL` is the string "LOW" where the SDK field is a `ThinkingLevel` enum (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — Every connect with a 3.8 thinking model raises a Pydantic serializer warning (seen again this run on `gemini-3.8-live-extended-thinking`); it also fires in `tests/test_gemini_live_interaction_status.py`. The wire value is right, so this is cosmetic. Importing `ThinkingLevel` at module scope would raise the effective google-genai floor above the `>=1.68.0` fallback pin, which the surrounding code deliberately avoids by feature-testing `ThinkingConfig.model_fields`. — *needs a call: whether to resolve `ThinkingLevel` defensively (getattr on the types module) or simply raise the google-genai floor*

Probed false this run on both the 3.8 and the 2.5 models. The page's default model is now correct, which closes last run's docs item. The page lives in the pipecat-docs repo, outside this one. — *needs a call: someone with the docs repo to drop the v1alpha note*
- [google/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Recognition features beyond the transcript flags are not exposed (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `diarization_config`, `multi_channel_mode`, `max_alternatives`, `transcript_normalization`, `translation_config`, `custom_prompt_config` (Chirp 3, preview), and language-agnostic recognition via `language_codes=["auto"]`, which today is reachable only through the deprecated `language_codes` field — *needs a call: which of these a voice-agent pipeline actually consumes — most produce data no downstream processor reads today*
- [google/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `markup` input and `advanced_voice_options` are not reachable from Settings (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — Both Cloud TTS classes always send `SynthesisInput(text=...)`/`StreamingSynthesisInput(text=...)`. `markup` carries Chirp 3 pause tags and Gemini structured markup (probed 2026-09-01: 197804 bytes against 121004 for plain text); `advanced_voice_options` carries `enable_textnorm`, `relax_safety_filters` and `safety_settings`. — *needs a call: pause control and custom pronunciations are Preview on Chirp 3 HD, and relaxed safety filters need a monthly-invoiced billing account - is a Settings field worth it for gated features?*
- [google/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/tts/2026-11-09.md) — GeminiTTSService's AVAILABLE_VOICES holds 30 names while the Gemini API voice library has hundreds, and the custom-ID exemption misses `voicekey_` IDs (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — The speech-generation reference documents 30 prebuilt names, Extended Voice Library entries from `GET /v1beta/voices`, `voice_...` IDs from voice design, replication and the voice remixing launched 2026-11-04, and stateless `voicekey_...` keys. Anything outside the hard-coded list logs "not in known voices list. Using anyway.", and the exemption tests `startswith("voice_")`, which `voicekey_...` does not match. - [google/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Gemini API audio output format and sample rate are not reachable; GeminiTTSService is fixed at 24 kHz PCM (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — The speech-generation reference documents `response_format.mime_type` (`audio/wav`, `audio/l16`, `audio/mulaw`, `audio/alaw`) and `sample_rate` (8000/16000/24000), which would let a telephony bot take 8 kHz mulaw straight from the model. The class hard-codes `GOOGLE_SAMPLE_RATE = 24000` and warns on anything else, and those knobs live on the dedicated `interactions` TTS surface, not on the `generate_content` config the service uses. — *needs a call: reaching them means calling the dedicated TTS endpoint instead of `generate_content_stream` - is a second GenAI request path worth 8 kHz telephony output?*

Host, model IDs and auth are unchanged - the probes pass - so this is docs drift only. The Vertex deprecations page still lists nothing but the 2025 Vertex AI SDK generative-AI module entry. — *needs a call: refresh the docstring link (and any docs.pipecat.ai wording) to the Agent Platform pages, or leave "Vertex" naming alone until Google retires the endpoint?*
- [google/vertex-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/vertex-llm/2026-11-09.md) — The location docstring says global is the only location serving the Gemini 3 series, but the multi-region endpoints now serve it (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — Google's Gemini 3.8 Flash model guide gives the supported regions as Global and Multi-region, and the default answers at `location="us"` (TTFAT 1013 ms). - [google/vertex-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/google/vertex-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — The Vertex override that suppresses `history_config` looks obsolete (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `GeminiLiveVertexLLMService._get_history_config` still returns None (added 2026-03-27); the 3.8 migration guide tells Vertex users to seed history with `initial_history_in_client_content=True`, and a Vertex 3.8 session recalled seeded history identically with and without the flag (2026-10-12) — *needs a call: whether the flag changes anything on Vertex's audio-input history-recall path — both checks seeded history as text, not audio*

Separately `_send_flush` sends `flush_id` as a string where the reference types it an integer — *needs a call: a call on whether to align our default with Gradium's 10, and confirmation the server keeps accepting a string flush_id*
- [gradium/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/gradium/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `run_tts`'s inline reconnect keeps stale entries in `_setup_context_ids` (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `_setup_context_ids` is cleared only in `_disconnect_websocket`. The `WebsocketService` reconnect path is fine — `_reconnect_websocket` calls `_disconnect_websocket` before reopening — but `run_tts`'s own `state is State.CLOSED` branch calls `_connect()` directly, so a context already set up on the dead socket keeps its id and the new socket receives `text` without a `setup`. Narrow window: ids are discarded on `end_of_stream`, and with the session cap at 3000 s it needs a socket drop mid-context. — *needs a call: whether a mid-context socket drop is worth handling here, or whether clearing the set in `_connect` is simply the right thing to do*
- [gradium/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/gradium/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `Settings.language` has no effect on synthesis (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — Gradium's TTS setup still has no language field — language follows the voice (and Voice Localization, which mints ordinary voice ids), and text normalization follows `rewrite_rules` in `json_config`. We default it to None and never send it, but Gradium's Pipecat page advertises `settings.language` as the synthesis language. — *needs a call: decide whether language should map onto `rewrite_rules` or be documented as unsupported for this service*
- [gradium/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/gradium/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Telephony and low-rate output formats are not used (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `output_format` is hard-coded to `pcm` (48 kHz) with `SAMPLE_RATE = 48000`; Gradium also offers `ulaw_8000` (alias `mulaw_8000`), `alaw_8000`, `pcm_8000`/`pcm_16000`/`pcm_22050`/`pcm_24000`/`pcm_44100`/`pcm_48000`, `wav` and `opus`, so a telephony pipeline pays for a 48 kHz downsample it could ask the server to skip. Gradium's telephony recipe says outright to request a format the bridge can play and avoid resampling. — *needs a call: whether asking Gradium for the transport's rate is worth diverging from the fixed 48 kHz the docs promise*
- [groq/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/groq/llm/2026-11-09.md) — GroqLLMService leaves Groq's reasoning default in place: gpt-oss-120b thinks on every turn (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — this run `reasoning_effort="low"` won both prompts — 319 ms TTFAT against 412 ms shipped on a trivial prompt (median of 5 each), and 448 ms against 736 ms on a reasoning-triggering one (median of 3 each, ranges 386–484 vs 670–797, non-overlapping). Across three runs `low` is ahead in four of six comparisons; only the 2026-10-28 trivial-prompt pair and the 2026-10-12 hard-prompt pair went the other way. — *needs a call: a call on whether GroqLLMService should ship `reasoning_effort="low"` — it changes default behavior, so it is a maintainer decision rather than an automatic bump*

- [groq/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/groq/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Groq's 10-second minimum billed length per request is not surfaced anywhere in Pipecat (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — Groq bills a 10-second minimum per transcription request; SegmentedSTTService sends one request per user utterance, and conversational turns are typically 1–3 s, so real cost is several times the audio duration. - [groq/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/groq/tts/2026-11-09.md) — No guard on Orpheus's documented 200-character input limit (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — Groq still documents that "The input text length is limited to 200 characters"; the 2026-09-14 run synthesized a 235-character request without complaint, and Pipecat's sentence aggregation routinely exceeds 200 characters — *needs a call: confirm whether over-limit input degrades quality before adding any splitting or warning — the API does not reject it*
