- [heygen/video](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/heygen/video/2026-11-09.md) — agent.speak_started / agent.speak_ended events are not handled (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — both fired in order again in today's session, each carrying the utterance id as `source_event_id`; TTFB instead keys off the first BotStartedSpeakingFrame decoded from the LiveKit track — *needs a call: whether avatar-speech metrics should come from these events rather than from the LiveKit track*
- [heygen/video](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/heygen/video/2026-11-09.md) — No handling for the per-caller rate limits LiveAvatar began enforcing on 2026-09-01 (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — 10 req/s per API key, and 5 req/s per session token on sessions start/stop/keep-alive; over-limit requests return 429 with Retry-After and X-RateLimit-* headers. `LiveAvatarApi._request` raises LiveAvatarApiError on any non-ok status, so a 429 during session setup surfaces as a setup failure rather than a retry. — *needs a call: whether a retry belongs in the service or in the application spawning sessions; a single bot never approaches the limit*
- [inception/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inception/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Inception's reasoning_summary / reasoning_summary_wait request fields are not reachable from Settings (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — the fields would pass through like `realtime` does, and responses now carry a `reasoning_summary` object with a `status`, but `BaseOpenAILLMService` reads nothing but `usage.completion_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens`, so a summary would have nowhere to go. `diffusing` is deliberately left out: streaming intermediate denoising steps would corrupt downstream sentence aggregation for TTS. — *needs a call: whether Pipecat wants reasoning summaries surfaced at all, which is a base-class question rather than an Inception one*
- [inception/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inception/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Mercury Voice, Inception's voice-agent model, has no published model ID (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — now on the models page with a 128K context, reasoning_effort low/medium/high and sub-170 ms TTFT, already running production voice agents at named customers — the most directly relevant model Inception ships for Pipecat. Still absent from the /models catalogue, no ID in the API reference, access by contacting sales. — *needs a call: nothing to do until it has a model ID a user can pass; re-check next run*
- [inception/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inception/llm/2026-11-09.md) — mercury-decide is in the catalogue but is not a chat model — it answers on `v1/decisions` with no chat, streaming or tool calling, so passing it as `model=` to InceptionLLMService cannot work; the catalogue listing makes it look available — *needs a call: whether a non-chat decisions endpoint is something Pipecat wants to wrap at all, which would be a new service rather than a change to this one*
- [inworld/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inworld/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — Per-modality usage in `response.done` (`usage.tts`, `usage.stt`, `usage.llm.model`, cached and reasoning token details) is dropped (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `_handle_evt_response_done` reads only `total/input/output_tokens` into `LLMTokenUsage`; a probe's `response.done` carried `llm`, `tts`, `input_token_details` and `output_token_details` alongside them, and those are the canonical billing signals for the cascade — *needs a call: which Pipecat metric a cascaded realtime service should report TTS/STT seconds and cached tokens under*
- [inworld/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inworld/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — `idle_timeout_ms` is settable but its server event `input_audio_buffer.timeout_triggered` is ignored — the merged session-fields PR added `TurnDetection.idle_timeout_ms`, so a user can arm the server idle timer, but `_server_event_types` has no `input_audio_buffer.timeout_triggered` entry and the receive loop drops it — nothing in the pipeline learns the user went quiet. The sibling smart-turn events `input_audio_buffer.turn_suggestion` / `.turn_suggestion_revoked` are unmodelled too — *needs a call: which Pipecat frame an idle timeout should become, given the service already proposes turn boundaries through `ProposedUserStoppedSpeakingFrame`*
- [inworld/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inworld/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `languageSettings.candidates` (the shortlist Inworld picks from when auto-detecting language) is not reachable — WebSocket create config only, up to 10 BCP-47 tags; the service sends a single `language` or nothing — *needs a call: whether a WebSocket-only language shortlist belongs on the shared Settings beside the existing single `language`*
- [inworld/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inworld/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The Pipecat docs page still offers `inworld-tts-1.5-max` / `inworld-tts-1.5-mini` and does not mention `inworld-tts-2-flash` (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — both 1.5 models are deprecated at Inworld; the page lives in the docs repo, not here — *needs a call: someone with access to the docs repo to update the model list*
- [inworld/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inworld/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `temperature=1.1` is inert in both Inworld examples (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — in `examples/realtime/realtime-ultravox-text.py` it is passed as a constructor argument that does not exist and is swallowed by `**kwargs`; in `examples/voice/voice-inworld.py` it reaches the request, but Inworld documents `temperature` as ignored on `inworld-tts-2` — *needs a call: whether the examples should drop `temperature` outright, given the default model ignores it, rather than move it onto `Settings`*
- [inworld/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/inworld/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `audioConfig.bitRate` and `timestampType: CHARACTER` are not reachable (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `bitRate` only matters for the compressed encodings the `encoding=` argument already allows (MP3, OGG_OPUS); `timestampType` is hard-coded to `WORD` — *needs a call: a call on whether either is worth surfacing, given word timestamps are what the word-timestamp plumbing consumes*
- [keenable/tool](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/keenable/tool/2026-11-09.md) — Keenable's new `session_id` is never sent, so an agent's calls are not grouped — added to both tools in November; Keenable asks for one value repeated across a session, which is a per-session identifier the wrapper could pin (and hide) rather than a knob a user tunes — left to the model today, it varies call to call or is omitted — *needs a call: whether the wrapper should mint and pin a session id by default, which changes what every call sends*
- [keenable/tool](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/keenable/tool/2026-11-09.md) — Keenable's integrator side channel `_meta["keenable/overrides"]` is unreachable (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — it is the documented way to pin `mode` and the only way to reach `skip_cache`; the wrapper pins `mode` as a tool argument instead, which the live schema still accepts, and `MCPClient._call_tool_text` passes no `meta=` to `session.call_tool` although mcp 2.3.0 still takes one — *needs a call: whether `skip_cache` is worth a request-`_meta` hook in MCPClient, given the argument path already pins the mode*
- [kokoro/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/kokoro/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The fp16/int8 and kokoro-v1.1-zh model variants can only be selected by downloading them by hand (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `Settings.model` is unused; the fp16 (164 MB) and int8 (114 MB) exports and the Chinese/English kokoro-v1.1-zh model with its 103-voice pack are reachable only through the `model_path`/`voices_path` constructor arguments. — *needs a call: whether a `model` value should map to a download URL pair, and whether the zh model's separate voice pack fits that mapping.*
- [kokoro/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/kokoro/tts/2026-11-09.md) — kokoro-onnx's `trim`, `sentence_pause` and `clause_pause` controls are not exposed (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — they tune the silence between synthesis batches, which is what a listener hears as sentence pacing. — *needs a call: not a plain pass-through — pauses are only inserted when `trim` is on, so the three settings have to be exposed as a group with that interaction documented.*
- [mem0/memory](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/mem0/memory/2026-11-09.md) — Service drives the synchronous Mem0 clients through `asyncio.to_thread` (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — mem0ai 2.x ships `AsyncMemoryClient` and `AsyncMemory`; using them would drop the thread hop on every context frame — *needs a call: a call on whether the churn is worth it, since `to_thread` already keeps the event loop free*
- [mem0/memory](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/mem0/memory/2026-11-09.md) — mem0 User Profiles (SDK 2.2.0) are not used to seed context — `MemoryClient.get_profile()`/`generate_profile()` return a schema-shaped summary of an entity's memories, refreshed about every 10 messages — a cheaper session-start alternative to the per-turn search this service runs. The feature is beta and must be enabled per project by mem0 support — *needs a call: whether a beta, opt-in-by-support feature belongs in the service at all, and if so whether a profile replaces or supplements the per-turn search results*
- [meta/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/meta/stt/2026-11-09.md) — The file-transcription endpoint has no Pipecat service (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — `POST https://api.meta.ai/v1/asr/transcribe` (multipart `request` + `audio`, WAV, 10 min / 32 MB cap) shares models, modes, `languageBias` and `keywords` with the realtime socket; it would fit a `SegmentedSTTService` subclass. — *needs a call: a call on whether a batch Meta STT class is wanted at all, given the realtime socket covers the voice-agent case*
- [minimax/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/minimax/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `timbre_weights` voice mixing is not reachable from Settings (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — up to four voice_id/weight pairs blended into one timbre; the T2A reference now labels the field legacy — *needs a call: a nested list-of-objects field on a field MiniMax calls legacy, so it needs a call on whether to expose it at all*
- [minimax/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/minimax/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Audio encoding is fixed at pcm/128 kbps/mono with no `force_cbr` or `output_format` (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `_audio_bitrate`, `_audio_format` and `_audio_channel` are init-only private attributes; the API also takes `force_cbr` and `output_format` — *needs a call: pcm is what the pipeline consumes, so exposing format at all needs a reason beyond parity with the API*
- [mistral/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/mistral/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Mistral-specific request parameters have no Settings fields — `prompt_cache_key`, `safe_prompt`, `parallel_tool_calls`, `response_format`, `stop`, `guardrails`, `prompt_mode` (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — correcting the previous report: plain `Settings.extra` does not reach the provider-only ones (`safe_prompt`, `guardrails`, `prompt_mode`) because the OpenAI client rejects unknown keyword arguments; they work only through `extra={"extra_body": {...}}`. `prompt_cache_key` is the one with a concrete payoff (Mistral bills cached prompt tokens at 10%). — *needs a call: whether `extra["extra_body"]` is the intended home for provider-specific knobs on OpenAI-compatible services, or whether the common ones earn typed fields.*
- [mistral/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/mistral/stt/2026-11-09.md) — `target_streaming_delay_ms` is constructor-only, not runtime-updatable (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — the main accuracy/latency knob (80–1200 ms, or 2400 ms, per session); `update_session` can change it mid-session, but the service only reads it at construction — *needs a call: moving it into Settings would duplicate the existing constructor argument, so it needs a stance on deprecating that argument*
- [mistral/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/mistral/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `language` is accepted in MistralTTSSettings but never sent (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — Mistral's speech endpoint still has no language parameter in 3.7.0; docs.pipecat.ai already describes the field as sentence-splitting only, while the `MistralTTSSettings` docstring says "Language for speech synthesis" — *needs a call: whether unsupported inherited fields should be documented as no-ops here or dropped from the docstring*
- [mistral/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/mistral/tts/2026-11-09.md) — No Pipecat service for the realtime TTS model voxtral-mini-tts-realtime-3-0 — the audio overview lists `voxtral-mini-tts-realtime-3-0` for live conversations and mistralai 3.7.0 ships `RealtimeSpeechSession` types; the model is absent from our account's /v1/models catalogue, and a websocket TTS service is a new class, not a settings change — *needs a call: whether a realtime Mistral TTS service is wanted at all, and whether the model is generally reachable yet*
- [moonshine/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/moonshine/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Speaker diarization (`identify_speakers`) is not reachable from Settings (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — diarization runs locally via the `identify_speakers` transcriber option plus `diarization_model_dir` from `get_diarization_model()` (an 8.2 MB download), filling `TranscriptLine.speaker_spans`; per-VAD-segment transcription makes it near-useless for a single-user turn, so it matters only for multi-speaker audio — *needs a call: `TranscriptionFrame` cannot carry speaker spans, so surfacing them needs a stance like Speechmatics' speaker-label formatting*
- [moonshine/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/moonshine/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Word timestamps are not requested or surfaced (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `get_model_for_language(..., include_word_timestamps=True)` plus the `word_timestamps` transcriber option fills `TranscriptLine.words`; the service asks for neither — *needs a call: Pipecat's `TranscriptionFrame` has nowhere to carry word timings, so this needs a frame-level decision first*
- [moonshine/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/moonshine/stt/2026-11-09.md) — moonshine-voice ships on-device TTS with no Pipecat service to reach it (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — the package exposes `list_tts_voices`, `get_tts_voice_catalog`, voice cloning and a streaming synthesizer; Pipecat has no `MoonshineTTSService`, so nothing in the inventory watches it — *needs a call: whether a local Moonshine TTS service is wanted next to Kokoro*
- [neuphonic/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/neuphonic/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The language map covers 10 of Neuphonic's 44 documented languages (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — the docs list 44 codes and the live voice catalogue carries 45 (it adds `ms`, which the docs omit); unmapped languages still reach the API through `resolve_language(..., use_base_code=True)` but log a warning — *needs a call: whether to hand-verify the remaining codes against a matching voice before adding them, since the map is meant to be the verified set*
- [neuphonic/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/neuphonic/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Default sample rate is 22050 while Neuphonic's documented default is 24000 (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — both classes hard-default `sample_rate=22050`; Neuphonic supports 8000/16000/22050/24000 and documents 24000 — *needs a call: raising the shipped default changes output for every existing user — is the quality worth it?*
- [neuphonic/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/neuphonic/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `Settings(model=...)` is silently ignored (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — the store sets `model=None` and no request carries a model; Neuphonic's docs say `voice_id` determines which models are used, so there is nothing to send — *needs a call: whether to reject a model in `Settings`, document it as unsupported, or leave the no-op*
- [novita/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/novita/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Novita's reasoning models need `chat_template_kwargs` to turn thinking off, reachable only via the `extra_body` nesting in `extra` (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `settings=Settings(extra={"extra_body": {"chat_template_kwargs": {"thinking": False}}})` probes green on kimi-k3.1-pro (2997 ms -> 1453 ms) and kimi-k3.1-pro-highspeed (2932 ms -> 1954 ms); nothing in the class docstring or docs.pipecat.ai says so — *needs a call: whether this belongs in the class docstring here or only in the docs repo*
- [nvidia/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/nvidia/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Catalogue lists nvidia/nemotron-nano-3-30b-a3b but the cloud endpoint does not serve it (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — re-probed this run, still 404; fifth failed probe across four runs — *needs a call: re-probe next run before treating it as a default candidate*
- [nvidia/omni-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/nvidia/omni-llm/2026-11-09.md) — No TTFAT metric in audio input mode — `service_metadata_frame()` reports `is_realtime_service=True` in audio input mode, which makes `LLMService.reports_ttfat` False, so no `TTFATMetricsData` is emitted even though Omni answers in text tokens; probed, the same prompt reports TTFAT 680 ms with `audio_input=False` and none with the default — *needs a call: whether the realtime flag should keep gating TTFAT for a service that answers in text rather than audio*
- [nvidia/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/nvidia/stt/2026-11-09.md) — The default boosted_lm_score of 4.0 is above the range 26.10.0 documents for RNNT and TDT models (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — 26.10.0 widened word-boosting scores to -200..200 for CTC models and Nemotron ASR Streaming but kept 0.5..2.0 for other RNNT and TDT models; both services default `boosted_lm_score` to 4.0, which is only in range on the hosted default. Probed again with `boosted_lm_words` set: the hosted Nemotron deployment accepts 4.0 — *needs a call: whether to lower the default or document it per model — the out-of-range case needs a self-hosted Parakeet TDT or RNNT NIM to observe*
- [nvidia/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/nvidia/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Nemotron Transcribe is not reachable from any NVIDIA STT class — 26.10.0's Nemotron Transcribe (40 languages, instruction-driven offline transcription, translation to and from English) is served only over an OpenAI-compatible HTTP API (`/v1/audio/transcriptions`, `/v1/chat/completions`), which NVIDIA's own ASR reference excludes from the gRPC and realtime docs both of our paths use. `OpenAISTTService` already takes a `base_url`, so a self-hosted deployment is reachable that way — *needs a call: whether NVIDIA Nemotron Transcribe deserves a class or docs of its own, or stays an `OpenAISTTService` base_url recipe*
- [nvidia/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/nvidia/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Zero-shot prompt transcript (`zero_shot_data.transcript`) is not exposed (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — the service sets `audio_prompt`, `encoding` and `quality` but leaves `transcript` (and `sample_rate_hz`) unset, both of which riva-client 2.28.0 accepts alongside the prompt audio — *needs a call: the transcript belongs with the constructor's `zero_shot_audio_prompt_file`, so exposing it means a new constructor parameter, and hosted zero-shot needs NVIDIA approval to probe*
- [openai/image](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/image/2026-11-09.md) — Images API `safety_identifier` is not reachable from Settings (since 2026-10-28, 1 week) — a plain optional string OpenAI uses for abuse monitoring, and the documented replacement for the deprecated `user` parameter; `SpeechifyTTSService` is the only Pipecat service that exposes one today — *needs a call: a call on whether per-user safety identifiers belong in each OpenAI service's Settings - the LLM, STT and TTS services accept the same parameter and none exposes it, so doing it here alone sets the pattern by accident*
- [openai/live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — Native Decisions delegation is not modeled (since 2026-10-28, 1 week) — a session can delegate straight to Decisions with `delegation = {"type": "decisions", "decisions": {"model": ..., "questions": [...]}}`; the service's delegation union is `ClientDelegationConfig | ResponsesDelegationConfig` and its constructor takes `ResponsesDelegation | ClientDelegation`, so the mode cannot be chosen. Answers arrive as `decision.event` correlated by `delegation_id`, which events.py also lacks. The Decisions API went GA on 2026-10-22 with gpt-6-luna — *needs a call: whether a bounded-choice backend is a mode Pipecat wants to own, given it needs a third delegation class plus decision-event routing*
- [openai/live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — Client delegation cannot delay the model's acknowledgement (since 2026-10-28, 1 week) — `delegation.client.acknowledgement_delay_ms` holds back the live model's automatic acknowledgement so the app can send a silent update first, which is exactly the window a Pipecat client-delegation backend needs before the model fills the silence. `events.ClientDelegationConfig` carries only `type` — *needs a call: whether the knob belongs on the `ClientDelegation` dataclass as a new field, or should be derived from the backend's own timing*
- [openai/live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — Input audio cannot be muted server-side (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — `session.input_audio.mute` / `.unmute` and their `muted` / `unmuted` acknowledgments are not in events.py, so the only way to stop the model listening is for the pipeline to stop forwarding audio, which leaves a gap in the session timeline rather than a mute the server acknowledges — *needs a call: whether a pipeline that withholds audio is good enough, and if not what the surface should be (a service method, or a frame the transport can trigger)*
- [openai/live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — The session audio format is always 24 kHz PCM16 (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — `events.AudioFormat` models `audio/pcm`, `audio/pcmu` and `audio/pcma`, but `_send_session_config` never sets `audio.format`; input is resampled to 24 kHz and `SpeechOutputAudioRawFrame` is hard-coded to `OPENAI_SAMPLE_RATE`. The server confirms the default, echoing `format: {type: audio/pcm, rate: 24000}` in every started session. A telephony bot therefore resamples 8 kHz G.711 both ways instead of letting the session carry it — *needs a call: whether an 8 kHz G.711 session is worth threading the chosen format through the input resampler and the output frame rate*
- [openai/live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — Stored sessions cannot be forked (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — a stored session is forked by connecting to `wss://api.openai.com/v1/live/sessions/{id}/fork` and sending `session.start` with overrides (`store`, Responses delegation settings, `audio.format`). Both halves are technically reachable today — `on_session_started` hands the app the `SessionResource`, whose `id` it keeps, and `base_url` is a constructor argument that can name the fork endpoint — so what is missing is a supported way to say "resume this session" — *needs a call: whether forking deserves a first-class surface (a `fork_from` argument, say) or whether pointing `base_url` at the fork endpoint is the documented answer*
- [openai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — tool_choice cannot name a specific function or MCP tool (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — ours is `Literal["auto", "none", "required"]` on both SessionProperties and ResponseProperties; GA's RealtimeToolChoiceConfig also takes an object forcing one function or MCP tool — *needs a call: whether forcing a tool is worth supporting on a realtime service, where the service drives responses itself*
- [openai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — A custom voice object cannot be passed to AudioOutput.voice (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `voice` is typed `str | None`, so every built-in voice works, but GA's `{"id": "voice_1234"}` custom-voice form does not. The prompt-to-voice voices OpenAI released on 2026-10-16 do not widen this — the spec says voices created from text prompts work only in Live; Realtime custom voices must come from audio samples — *needs a call: whether Pipecat exposes OpenAI custom voices at all — no other OpenAI service in the repo does*
- [openai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — The documented OpenAI-Safety-Identifier connection header has no supported setting (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — PR 6329 moved the connection headers into an overridable `_auth_headers()` hook (AzureRealtimeLLMService already overrides it), so a subclass can add the header without copying `_connect`. The hook is private and undocumented, so there is still no configuration path. OpenAI's WebSocket guide sends `OpenAI-Safety-Identifier` next to `Authorization` in every example, and probing in an earlier run showed the API accepts it (full turn, TTFB 477 ms) — *needs a call: whether the private hook is answer enough, or the identifier wants a named setting — the automatic-PR rules exclude a new constructor argument, and a Settings field would be the first that configures the connection rather than the session*
- [openai/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — Out-of-band responses cannot be built — ResponseProperties has no input or conversation field — `send_client_event` is public, so a user can send a `ResponseCreateEvent`, but `ResponseProperties` models only seven fields and GA's response-create params also take `conversation` ("none" for an out-of-band response) and `input` (a fresh item list for this response alone). openai 3.41.0 (2026-10-28) widened that `input` to accept `{"type": "item_reference", "id": ...}` alongside full items, so a side-channel response can now cite items already in the session instead of repeating them — *needs a call: whether out-of-band responses are a pattern Pipecat wants to support on a service that drives its own responses, and if so whether they belong on ResponseProperties or on a dedicated API*
- [openai/responses-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/responses-llm/2026-11-09.md) — Responses request fields text.verbosity, prompt_cache_key/prompt_cache_retention/prompt_cache_options, context_management, image.context_compression, truncation, tool_choice, parallel_tool_calls, max_tool_calls, moderation and reasoning.context have no named Settings field (since 2026-08-25, 10 weeks) — `extra` is merged into the request verbatim at the end of `_build_response_params`, so this is discoverability, not capability. New this run: `image.context_compression` (auto / enabled / disabled), which governs how image context is compressed and is the one most likely to matter to the vision examples. `reasoning.context` remains the one that is not a clean pass-through, since it interacts with the encrypted-reasoning round-trip — *needs a call: a stance on whether these become named fields or `extra` stays the documented answer for Responses-only knobs*
- [openai/responses-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/responses-llm/2026-11-09.md) — service_tier is a constructor argument only, so the tier cannot be changed at runtime (since 2026-08-25, 10 weeks) — Ultrafast is access-controlled per model and OpenAI lets an organization restrict which tiers its projects may use, so picking a tier per model means rebuilding the service; every other request knob lives in Settings — *needs a call: whether service_tier should move into Settings, which would leave two ways to set it*
- [openai/responses-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/responses-llm/2026-11-09.md) — gpt-6-astra rejects temperature and top_p, and the service passes them through without warning (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — setting temperature on gpt-6-astra returns 400 "Unsupported parameter". Only users who set these are affected (both default to NOT_GIVEN); the service already has the `_warn_if_reasoning_unsupported` pattern for exactly this kind of per-model constraint — *needs a call: whether per-model parameter support is worth tracking in the service, or the provider's own error message is good enough*
- [openai/responses-llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/responses-llm/2026-11-09.md) — The approval-gated reasoning models gpt-6-rosalind and gpt-6-cyber have no reasoning-defaults entry, and OpenAI publishes no effort list for either — both are Responses models released since the last run (gpt-6-rosalind 2026-10-30 for life sciences, gpt-6-cyber 2026-11-05 for vulnerability work) and both need trusted-access approval, so neither appears in our catalogue and neither can be probed. gpt-6-cyber's model page states reasoning-token support but no efforts; gpt-6-rosalind has no public model page. A bot that selects either runs at OpenAI's medium default and logs the unlisted-model warning — *needs a call: whether approval-gated specialist models belong in the table at all, given no published effort list and no way to verify a value*
- [openai/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/stt/2026-11-09.md) — OpenAISTTService does not use the Audio API's streaming transcription responses (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — the speech-to-text guide still lists stream=true for gpt-transcribe, emitting transcript.text.delta and a final transcript.text.done; the service awaits the whole response, so a segment's ~810 ms TTFB is all dead time. OpenAIRealtimeSTTService already covers users who want streaming partials — *needs a call: whether SegmentedSTTService should push InterimTranscriptionFrame frames from a streamed Audio API response, which is more than a Settings field*
- [openai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Default voice is alloy while OpenAI's guide recommends marin or cedar (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — both are already in the allowlist and marin probed fine in an earlier run; alloy is not deprecated — *needs a call: a call on changing what every existing OpenAI TTS user hears by default*
- [openrouter/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openrouter/llm/2026-11-09.md) — `plugins` is not reachable from Settings (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — a pass-through array covering auto-router, auto-beta-router, pareto-router, switchyard-router, jev-router, moderation, web, web-fetch, file-parser, response-healing, context-compression, fusion, alignment and armor. Within it `cost_quality_tradeoff` stays deprecated in favour of `cost_tier` — *needs a call: whether a plugin list belongs in a voice pipeline at all, or stays an `extra` escape-hatch feature*
- [openrouter/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openrouter/llm/2026-11-09.md) — OpenRouter's caching and sticky-routing fields are not reachable from Settings (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — `prompt_cache_options` (`mode: explicit` plus a `ttl` of `30m` or `12h`, supported on OpenAI GPT-5.6 and newer), `prompt_cache_key`, `cache_control` and `session_id` bear on cost and latency as a call's context grows; all reach the API today only through `extra={"extra_body": {...}}` — *needs a call: whether prompt caching is worth first-class fields here rather than in the OpenAI base settings all compatible services share*
- [openrouter/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/openrouter/llm/2026-11-09.md) — A docs page example model slug is not in OpenRouter's catalogue (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — the page's "With a Different Provider Model" example names `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4`, which `GET /models` does not list -- OpenRouter's oldest Sonnet is `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5`. Its other examples, `openai/gpt-4o` and `google/gemini-2.5-pro`, are live — *needs a call: the page lives in pipecat-ai/docs, so it cannot be fixed from this repo*
- [piper/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/piper/tts/2026-11-09.md) — SynthesisConfig's volume and normalize_audio are not reachable from Settings (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — both are in-process only; Piper's /synthesize body has no equivalent — *needs a call: exposing them would make the two Piper settings classes asymmetric — a call on whether that is acceptable*
- [piper/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/piper/tts/2026-11-09.md) — The piper extra pins requests, which piper-tts no longer uses (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — piper-tts 1.8.0 declares no requests dependency in any extra; nothing under piper/ imports it, and download_voices uses urllib — *needs a call: confirm nothing else in the Piper path needs requests before dropping the pin*
- [pocket_tts/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/pocket_tts/tts/2026-11-09.md) — An unsupported language fails at construction with a FileNotFoundError (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — `language_to_pocket_tts_language(Language.JA)` returns `"ja"`, which `load_model` turns into a missing `ja.yaml` — *needs a call: needs a call on whether to raise an error naming the supported languages or to fall back to English*

- [qwen/llm](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/qwen/llm/2026-11-09.md) — Two examples pin qwen3.6-27b (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — examples/function-calling/function-calling-qwen.py and examples/update-settings/llm/llm-qwen.py both pin qwen3.6-27b; the model still serves and is on no retirement list. aiewf-eval rejudged both rows under its 2026-10-19 recovery-context migration — qwen3.8-27b 96.0% and qwen3.6-27b 92.1% on aiwf_medium_context, both thinking off — so the successor still scores higher, by a wider margin than the previous report's 98.2 / 97.3 — *needs a call: whether examples track the newest dense model, and it carries the same thinking-off question*
- [resembleai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/resembleai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `voice_settings_preset_uuid` (pace, pitch, temperature, exaggeration, useHd, style description) is not reachable from Settings (since 2026-09-14, 8 weeks) — dropped from medium — the capability is unchanged (the WebSocket reference still omits the field while the endpoint reads it; an unowned but well-formed preset UUID returns a 401 naming "invalid voice settings preset"), but building new request knobs for a product the provider has stopped selling is hygiene now rather than a capability users will ask for — *needs a call: a preset UUID in the probe account to confirm a valid one synthesizes, plus a stance on presets overriding any SSML a user already puts in the text*
- [resembleai/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/resembleai/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `project_uuid` is documented as required but never sent (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — Resemble's OpenAPI spec marks `project_uuid` "Optional project UUID to store the clip" on both HTTP synthesis endpoints, so the WebSocket page's "required" is a doc inconsistency rather than a request Resemble will start rejecting; synthesis succeeds without it (probe below) and clips land in the account's default project, so what is missing is the ability to choose one — *needs a call: a call on whether to expose the project at all, and a project UUID to probe with — none is available in the probe environment*

- [rime/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/rime/stt/2026-11-09.md) — RimeSTTService is not documented on docs.pipecat.ai — the unit's `docs_url` (`/api-reference/server/services/stt/rime`) returns 404 while the sibling Rime TTS and Deepgram STT pages return 200. - [rime/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/rime/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `RimeTTSSettings` docstrings label mist-era fields "(mistv2 only)" but the service sends them for every non-coda model, and `speedAlpha` reaches Coda too (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — Rime's Mist v2 reference says Coda and Mist v3 invert the `speedAlpha` convention relative to Mist v2, and the custom-pronunciation page scopes `phonemizeBetweenBrackets` to English Mist v3. Coda accepts `speedAlpha` without error. — *needs a call: Rime's own pages disagree on which models take these fields, and Coda's documented speed control is `timeScaleFactor` — correcting the labels needs a ruling on what the service should send to Coda*

A caller can already pass it as `default_headers={"x-session-affinity": ...}` at construction, which fits Pipecat's one-service-per-session shape. — *needs a call: whether the existing `default_headers` route is enough, or the service should mint and send a session ID itself*

- [sarvam/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/sarvam/stt/2026-11-09.md) — `with_timestamps` is not reachable from SarvamHttpSTTService — Sarvam's REST reference documents `with_timestamps`, which adds chunk-level (sentence/phrase) offsets to the response; `SarvamHttpSTTSettings` is empty and `_transcribe()` never sends it, while the realtime class exposes the equivalent `return_timestamps`. The offsets would ride along in the frame's `result`, since `run_stt()` already attaches the raw response — *needs a call: whether chunk offsets mean anything for a segment longer than 30 seconds, which the service splits into pieces whose offsets each restart at zero*

- [smallest/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/smallest/tts/2026-11-09.md) — `auto` language routing is not reachable through the Language enum (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — the language enum still lists `auto` first, for per-request detection and cross-language routing on any English or Hindi voice; users can only get it by passing the raw string, since Language has no AUTO member — *needs a call: whether to add a Language.AUTO member or document the raw-string escape hatch is a framework-wide call, not a Smallest one*

- [soniox/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/soniox/tts/2026-11-09.md) — Soniox caps output at 2 minutes per stream, and the rest of a longer turn is dropped silently (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — one turn context maps to one Soniox stream_id, and the limits page still states the 2-minute stream duration as fixed and not raisable; a turn that passes it gets a 413 `max_audio_duration_reached` and a `terminated`, after which the receive loop removes the audio context and later sentences of the same turn re-open the stream but have their audio discarded — *needs a call: whether a turn should roll onto a fresh stream_id at the cap (Soniox's own advice is to split the text across requests) or whether the limit should just be documented; not probed, since confirming it means synthesizing two minutes of audio*

- [speechmatics/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/speechmatics/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Language and output-locale maps are still behind the provider's list (since 2026-08-31, 10 weeks) — `BASE_LANGUAGES` omits `ia` and the bilingual packs `ar_en`, `en_ms`, `cmn_en`, `cmn_en_ms_ta`, `en_ta`, all re-confirmed on the provider's language page this run and one of which (`ar_en`) Agent STT accepted on the wire. `LOCALES` covers only en-GB/en-US/en-AU, so Mandarin's Traditional/Simplified output locales are unreachable too. `resolve_language(use_base_code=True)` lets an unmapped code through with a warning, so these are reachable as raw strings rather than blocked. — *needs a call: none of these codes has a `Language` enum member, so closing the gap means adding enum members, not table rows*
- [speechmatics/stt](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/speechmatics/stt/2026-11-09.md) — Speaker identifiers need a manual `GetSpeakers`, so `on_speakers_result` never fires on its own (since 2026-10-12, 4 weeks) — The last field of the documented Agent STT schema the service cannot express. `SpeakersResult` arrives only when `speaker_diarization_config.get_speakers` is true or the client sends a `GetSpeakers` message; the service subscribes to `SPEAKERS_RESULT` and registers `on_speakers_result` whenever diarization is on but asks for neither, and the `known_speakers` docstring says identifiers are "obtained from a running STT session". `send_message(ClientMessageType.GET_SPEAKERS, final=True)` is the working route; the SDK's `SpeakerDiarizationConfig` still has no `get_speakers` member in 0.3.0, re-checked on the installed package. — *needs a call: whether to add a `get_speakers` setting (which the SDK dataclass still cannot express) or just document the `send_message` route on the event handler*

Every probe in this report used a paced copy. - [ultravox/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/ultravox/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — probe.py cannot construct `UltravoxRealtimeLLMService`, so the unit has no tier-2 probe (since 2026-09-01, 9 weeks) — the constructor takes a required `params` object rather than `api_key`/`model`, so `build_kwargs` raises TypeError ("missing 1 required keyword-only argument: 'params'") and every run needs an ad-hoc script to connect — *needs a call: a probe.py change (params-object support in `build_kwargs`) is watch tooling, not a service change*

- [azure/realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/realtime/2026-11-09.md) — AZURE_REALTIME_BASE_URL names a resource host that does not exist (getaddrinfo fails for it while other hosts resolve fine), and its path is still the retired `/openai/realtime?api-version=...&deployment=...` preview form, so no connect probe could run.
- [azure/voice_live-realtime](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/azure/voice_live-realtime/2026-11-09.md) — No Voice Live credential in this environment (AZURE_VOICE_LIVE_ENDPOINT and AZURE_VOICE_LIVE_API_KEY unset), so nothing could be probed live.
- [bland/tts](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports/blob/main/reports/bland/tts/2026-11-09.md) — BLAND_API_KEY is not set, so no live probe could run

---
To record a decision about a change to consider, reply on this issue naming the service and enough of the item to identify it, one decision per line — e.g. `deepgram/stt, diarize_model: skip, the extra= workaround is fine` or `openai/realtime, tool_choice: tracked in #5400, stop reporting`. Fixes need no comment: the next run sees the code change. It reads these comments for everything it cannot see — won't do, later, tracked elsewhere — and records each decision in the service's `decisions.md`, beside its reports. Full triage workflow: the [README](https://github.com/pipecat-ai/provider-watch-reports#the-weekly-loop).
